<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.kimi.com/blog/kimi-k3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi K3: Open Frontier Intelligence</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3：开放前沿情报</span></a><span class="dom">kimi.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kimi unveils K3 as an Open Frontier Intelligence platform, outlining its architecture and potential use cases in open data exploration.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi 发表 K3，作为一个开放前沿情报平台，概述了其架构与在开放数据探索中的潜在应用场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kimi unveils K3 as an Open Frontier Intelligence platform, outlining its architecture and potential use cases in open data exploration. It emphasizes agent-driven workflows and scalable data access, signaling a push toward more transparent, configurable intelligence tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Kimi 发表 K3，作为一个开放前沿情报平台，概述了其架构与在开放数据探索中的潜在应用场景。强调代理驱动的工作流与可扩展的数据访问，预示向更透明、可配置的情报工具迈进。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>2066</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48935342' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1189</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48968606" data-id="48968606" data-tags="Show HN|Hardware|Open Source" data-score="1900" data-comments="200" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606" data-time="1784472076">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I replaced a $120k bowling center system with $1,600 in ESP32s</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 1,600 美元的 ESP32 替代 12 万美元的保龄球馆系统</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k.</span><span class='zh'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k. The project demonstrates how commodity microcontrollers and open-source software can replace expensive, vendor-locked solutions in real-world venues. It also invites evaluation of trade-offs around reliability, maintenance, and support in commercial deployments.</span><span class='zh'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。此项目展示了商用场景中，普通微控制器与开源软件仍有能力替代昂贵的、厂商绑定的解决方案。亦需权衡可靠性、维护与售后支持等现实挑战。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>11.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1900</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>200</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48889637" data-id="48889637" data-tags="AI" data-score="1493" data-comments="761" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889637" data-time="1783931979">
<a class="thumb" href="https://raymyers.org/post/zed-creator-calls-spade-a-spade/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://raymyers.org/weblog-card-1.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='raymyers.org' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://raymyers.org/post/zed-creator-calls-spade-a-spade/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zig Creator Calls Spade a Spade, Anthropic Blows Smoke</span><span class='zh'>Zig 创作者直言不讳，Anthropic 敷衍其词</span></a><span class="dom">raymyers.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An outspoken Zig language creator takes a blunt stance on AI safety claims associated with Anthropic, challenging the industry’s framing and transparency.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 语言的创作者对 Anthropic 的 AI 安全说法发表直言，挑战行业的表述与透明度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An outspoken Zig language creator takes a blunt stance on AI safety claims associated with Anthropic, challenging the industry’s framing and transparency. The controversy underscores the tension between ambitious safety promises and practical concerns from developers who build on AI tools. It highlights how independent voices can shape public scrutiny of AI vendors.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 语言的创作者对 Anthropic 的 AI 安全说法发表直言，挑战行业的表述与透明度。此事凸显了安全承诺的宏大叙事与开发者对 AI 工具的实际关切之间的张力。独立声音可能影响对 AI 供应商的公众监督。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>1493</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889637' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>761</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48945241" data-id="48945241" data-tags="Business" data-score="1276" data-comments="744" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241" data-time="1784281325">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AWS: Inaccurate Estimated Billing Data – $1.7 billion</span><span class='zh'>AWS：估算账单数据不准确，涉额17亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Amazon Web Services disclosed inaccuracies in its estimated billing data, leading to charges totaling about $1.7B across customers.</span><span class='zh'>云成本监控在大规模云环境中暴露出不足，估算账单数据存在不准确之处，涉案金额约17亿美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Amazon Web Services disclosed inaccuracies in its estimated billing data, leading to charges totaling about $1.7B across customers. The incident highlights how cloud-cost visibility can fail at scale and underscores the need for robust auditing, refund mechanisms, and better tooling for cost governance in large cloud environments.</span><span class='zh'>云成本监控在大规模云环境中暴露出不足，估算账单数据存在不准确之处，涉案金额约17亿美元。此事凸显需要更强的成本可视化、审计与退款流程，并推动改进的成本管理工具与治理方法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1276</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>744</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48924912" data-id="48924912" data-tags="AI" data-score="1196" data-comments="285" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48924912" data-time="1784139165">
<a class="thumb" href="https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/introducing-inkling/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/introducing-inkling/images/cover-social-inkling-post.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='thinkingmachines.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/introducing-inkling/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Inkling: Our Open-Weights Model</span><span class='zh'>Inkling：我们的开源权重模型</span></a><span class="dom">thinkingmachines.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Inkling is Thinking Machines&#x27; open-weights model.</span><span class='zh'>Inkling 是 Thinking Machines 的开源权重模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Inkling is Thinking Machines&#x27; open-weights model. It signals a broader push toward transparent, testable architectures. Open weights enable researchers to compare methods and stress-test safety features, potentially accelerating progress while requiring thoughtful governance.</span><span class='zh'>Inkling 是 Thinking Machines 的开源权重模型。这体现了对透明、可测试架构的推进。开源权重便于研究者对比方法、测试安全性，但也需要谨慎治理以防滥用。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://videocardz.com/newz/lg-monitors-silently-install-software-through-windows-update-without-user-consent" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.videocardz.com/1/2026/07/LG-MONITORS-MCAFFE.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='videocardz.com' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://videocardz.com/newz/lg-monitors-silently-install-software-through-windows-update-without-user-consent" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LG monitors silently install software through Windows Update without consent</span><span class='zh'>LG显示器通过Windows更新在未征得同意的情况下悄悄安装软件</span></a><span class="dom">videocardz.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LG monitors were found to silently install companion software through Windows Update without explicit user consent.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，LG显示器通过Windows Update在未明确同意的情况下安装附带软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LG monitors were found to silently install companion software through Windows Update without explicit user consent. The bundled software can run in the background and may raise privacy and security concerns, creating a potential for bloat. Readers should review update offers, remove unwanted components, and consider blocking vendor software when possible.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，LG显示器通过Windows Update在未明确同意的情况下安装附带软件。该软件可能后台运行，带来隐私与安全风险，并可能造成系统臃肿。读者应在更新时谨慎勾选、移除不需要的组件，并在可行时阻止厂商软件推送。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1148</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956688' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>581</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48886741" data-id="48886741" data-tags="AI" data-score="1049" data-comments="449" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48886741" data-time="1783905860">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48886741" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48886741" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: Add flag for AI-generated articles</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：为 AI 生成的文章加上标记</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A discussion about introducing a flag to label AI-generated content on HN.</span><span class='zh'>讨论在 Hacker News 上为 AI 生成的文章添加标记的可行性与影响。</span></p>

It weighs transparency, reader trust, and the potential impact on ranking, moderation, and author accountability.</span><span class='zh'>讨论在 Hacker News 上为 AI 生成的文章添加标记的可行性与影响。核心在于提升透明度与读者信任，同时考虑对排序、审核和作者责任的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fabiensanglard.net/jurrasic_park_computers/index.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jurassic Park computers in excruciating detail</span><span class='zh'>《侏罗纪公园》中的计算机：详尽到极致</span></a><span class="dom">fabiensanglard.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This piece dissects the film&#x27;s on-screen computer systems with an eye for hardware, OS choices, and faux interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>本文逐步拆解《侏罗纪公园》中的银幕计算机，揭示道具团队如何选用真实硬件与界面语言来营造可信感，并分析这对当代电影界面设计的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This piece dissects the film&#x27;s on-screen computer systems with an eye for hardware, OS choices, and faux interfaces. It reveals how the prop-makers staged computing to look convincing on 1993-era tech, shaping viewers&#x27; perception of digital authority. The analysis also explains why these UI aesthetics continue to influence cinematic design.</span><span class='zh'>本文逐步拆解《侏罗纪公园》中的银幕计算机，揭示道具团队如何选用真实硬件与界面语言来营造可信感，并分析这对当代电影界面设计的影响。作者还讨论其对后续影视作品在呈现技术方面的影响力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/2026/07/16/microsoft-comic-chat-is-now-open-source/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft Comic Chat is now open source</span><span class='zh'>微软 Comic Chat 现已开源</span></a><span class="dom">opensource.microsoft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft released the classic Comic Chat client as open source, reviving a piece of 1990s online chat UI and protocol.</span><span class='zh'>微软将经典的 Comic Chat 客户端开源，重现了 1990 年代的在线聊天界面和协议。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft released the classic Comic Chat client as open source, reviving a piece of 1990s online chat UI and protocol. The release provides a sandbox for experimentation with vintage software, preserving artifacts and teaching UX evolution.</span><span class='zh'>微软将经典的 Comic Chat 客户端开源，重现了 1990 年代的在线聊天界面和协议。此次发布为复古软件的实验、保存与研究提供了机会，也揭示了聊天用户体验的演变。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pigeonsandplanes.com/read/music-piracy-what-cd-oink-nine-inch-nails-streaming" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The lost joy of music piracy</span><span class='zh'>音乐盗版的逝去乐趣</span></a><span class="dom">pigeonsandplanes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Streaming turned music into a predictable, licensed product, draining the anarchic joy of early piracy.</span><span class='zh'>流媒体让音乐变成可控的授权商品，削弱了早期盗版带来的狂热和社区感。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Streaming turned music into a predictable, licensed product, draining the anarchic joy of early piracy. The piece reflects on how the pirate-era culture created camaraderie and discovery, and asks what we lose when access becomes commodified.</span><span class='zh'>流媒体让音乐变成可控的授权商品，削弱了早期盗版带来的狂热和社区感。本文回顾那段盗版文化如何塑造了发现与分享的乐趣，并讨论当下更易获取的市场会让我们错过哪些宝贵的文化体验。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A practical take on web vs native apps: many projects can be delivered as a robust webpage, reducing maintenance and improving accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>谈及网页与原生应用的现实取舍：很多项目其实可以用强大的网页版本来实现，降低维护成本并提升可访问性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A practical take on web vs native apps: many projects can be delivered as a robust webpage, reducing maintenance and improving accessibility. The post shows concrete steps to flatten typical app functionality into a responsive web UI.</span><span class='zh'>谈及网页与原生应用的现实取舍：很多项目其实可以用强大的网页版本来实现，降低维护成本并提升可访问性。文中给出将常见应用功能转换成响应式网页界面的具体做法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tech.supercarblondie.com/japan-recovers-up-to-90-of-lithium-from-used-ev-batteries/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Japan develops a method to recover up to 90% of lithium from used EV batteries</span><span class='zh'>日本开发可从废旧新能源汽车电池回收高达90%的锂</span></a><span class="dom">tech.supercarblondie.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Japan has demonstrated a process capable of recovering up to 90% of lithium from spent EV batteries, boosting recycling efficiency and extending battery supply resilience.</span><span class='zh'>日本展示了一种可从废旧电动车电池中回收锂高达90%的工艺，显著提升回收效率并加强电池材料保障。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Japan has demonstrated a process capable of recovering up to 90% of lithium from spent EV batteries, boosting recycling efficiency and extending battery supply resilience. The advance could lower material costs and lessen environmental impact by reducing mining and refining needs.</span><span class='zh'>日本展示了一种可从废旧电动车电池中回收锂高达90%的工艺，显著提升回收效率并加强电池材料保障。此举可能降低成本、减轻采矿与精炼带来的环境压力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://workshop.cjpais.com/projects/transcribe-cpp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Transcribe.cpp</span><span class='zh'>Transcribe.cpp 实现</span></a><span class="dom">workshop.cjpais.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Transcribe.cpp presents a compact C++ project that demonstrates a speech-to-text workflow.</span><span class='zh'>Transcribe.cpp 展示了一个精简的 C++ 转写实现，展示了将音频转换为文本的基本流程，并讨论了延迟与准确率之间的权衡，包含流式与批量转写的取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Transcribe.cpp presents a compact C++ project that demonstrates a speech-to-text workflow. It walks through building a lightweight transcription pipeline and discusses latency versus accuracy trade-offs, including how to structure streaming versus batch transcription.</span><span class='zh'>Transcribe.cpp 展示了一个精简的 C++ 转写实现，展示了将音频转换为文本的基本流程，并讨论了延迟与准确率之间的权衡，包含流式与批量转写的取舍。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://academic.oup.com/sleep/article/47/1/zsad253/7280269" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#4ade80' data-tag='Health'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=academic.oup.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>academic.oup.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://academic.oup.com/sleep/article/47/1/zsad253/7280269" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sleep regularity is a stronger predictor of mortality risk than sleep duration (2023)</span><span class='zh'>睡眠规律性比睡眠时长更能预测死亡风险</span></a><span class="dom">academic.oup.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New research suggests that how regularly you sleep predicts mortality risk more strongly than total sleep duration.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究表明，睡眠时间的规律性与死亡风险的相关性比总睡眠时长更强。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New research suggests that how regularly you sleep predicts mortality risk more strongly than total sleep duration. Using long-term wearable data, the study links consistent sleep timing with better health outcomes, independent of hours slept. The finding shifts emphasis toward regular schedules as a health strategy and dovetails with consumer wellness tech.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究表明，睡眠时间的规律性与死亡风险的相关性比总睡眠时长更强。通过长期可穿戴设备数据，研究发现稳定的睡眠时序与更好的健康结果相关，且与实际睡眠时长无关。该发现将健康策略的重点从睡眠时长转向规律作息，并与可穿戴健康科技的发展相吻合。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mixfont.com/experiments/decoy-font" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decoy Font</span><span class='zh'>Decoy 字体</span></a><span class="dom">mixfont.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Decoy Font is an experimental typeface designed to confuse automated readers and OCR systems by mapping glyphs in unusual ways.</span><span class='zh'>Decoy 字体是一种实验性字体，通过以异常的方式映射字形来混淆自动识别系统与 OCR。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Decoy Font is an experimental typeface designed to confuse automated readers and OCR systems by mapping glyphs in unusual ways. It highlights typography’s impact on AI text detectors and reveals current recognition limits.</span><span class='zh'>Decoy 字体是一种实验性字体，通过以异常的方式映射字形来混淆自动识别系统与 OCR。它揭示排版对 AI 文本检测的影响与当前识别能力的边界。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://systima.ai/blog/claude-code-vs-opencode-token-overhead" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code sends 33k tokens before reading the prompt; OpenCode sends 7k</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取提示前消耗 3.3 万令牌；OpenCode 仅约 7 千令牌</span></a><span class="dom">systima.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens. The gap highlights how different toolchains incur context overhead that affects latency, cost, and developer workflows in AI-assisted coding. This emphasizes the importance of managing token budgets when chaining multiple AI services.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。这一差距揭示了不同工具链在上下文开销上的差异，进而影响 AI 辅助编码的延迟、成本和工作流。强调在串联多家 AI 服务时对令牌预算进行管理的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://prismml.com/news/bonsai-27b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bonsai 27B: A 27B-Class model that runs on a phone</span><span class='zh'>Bonsai 27B：能在手机上运行的27B级模型</span></a><span class="dom">prismml.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Bonsai 27B project demonstrates a 27-billion-parameter model that can run directly on a smartphone, signaling a practical edge-AI milestone.</span><span class='zh'>Bonsai 27B 展示了一个27B参数级模型能够直接在手机上运行，标志着边缘AI的一个实际里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Bonsai 27B project demonstrates a 27-billion-parameter model that can run directly on a smartphone, signaling a practical edge-AI milestone. Achieving on-device inference requires aggressive optimization (pruning, quantization, efficient runtimes) to fit memory and power constraints, but it promises lower latency and stronger privacy. This shift hints at a future where powerful AI can operate without constant cloud access.</span><span class='zh'>Bonsai 27B 展示了一个27B参数级模型能够直接在手机上运行，标志着边缘AI的一个实际里程碑。实现本地推理需要对模型进行大幅优化（裁剪、量化、高效运行时），以适应内存与电池限制，但可带来更低的延迟和更强的隐私保护。这预示着强大AI有望在不依赖云端的情况下运行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://old.reddit.com/r/math/comments/1uxj3cy/after_openais_cdc_proof_announcement_gpt56_used_a/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-5.6 used a prompt to close a 30-year gap in convex optimization</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.6 用提示弥补凸优化三十年难题</span></a><span class="dom">old.reddit.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A prompt reportedly closed a 30-year gap in convex optimization after a CDC-style proof announcement.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GPT-5.6 通过一个提示，解决了凸优化领域长达30年的未解难题，这一成果在 CDC 风格证明公告后出现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A prompt reportedly closed a 30-year gap in convex optimization after a CDC-style proof announcement. If verified, it demonstrates how carefully crafted prompts can guide AI to tackle deep mathematical problems and produce testable steps. The claim invites scrutiny of reproducibility and the proper role of AI in formal proofs.</span><span class='zh'>据称，GPT-5.6 通过一个提示，解决了凸优化领域长达30年的未解难题，这一成果在 CDC 风格证明公告后出现。若属实，表明大型语言模型在帮助人类数学家进行问题表述和证据推导方面具有潜在价值，同时也需要对可重复性与独立验证保持警惕。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">25</div>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SpaceX bonds trade roughly 10% below issue price, signaling waning investor demand or rising perceived risk.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 债券交易价格约较发行价低约10%，反映市场对其风险的重新定价或需求走弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SpaceX bonds trade roughly 10% below issue price, signaling waning investor demand or rising perceived risk. If the debt slides toward junk status, SpaceX could face higher refinancing costs and tighter liquidity windows as macro headwinds bite tech funding. This serves as a reminder that even highly popular tech issuers are not immune to debt-market sentiment.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 债券交易价格约较发行价低约10%，反映市场对其风险的重新定价或需求走弱。若债务评级进一步恶化，再融资成本将上升，流动性可能收紧，凸显当前宏观环境对科技股相关债务的冲击。此事再次印证即使是备受关注的科技发行也会受债市情绪影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://petapixel.com/2026/07/16/mayor-mamdani-says-landlords-cant-secretly-use-ai-images-to-advertise-properties/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NYC may require landlords and realtors to disclose the use of AI in listings</span><span class='zh'>纽约市可能要求房东披露在房源信息中使用 AI 的情况</span></a><span class="dom">petapixel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New York City is considering regulations to require landlords and real estate agents to disclose the use of AI-generated imagery in listings.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市正考虑立法，要求房东和中介披露房源照片中是否使用了 AI 生成的图像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New York City is considering regulations to require landlords and real estate agents to disclose the use of AI-generated imagery in listings. The move aims to curb deceptive advertising and increase transparency in online property listings, with implications for proptech platforms and compliance burdens.</span><span class='zh'>纽约市正考虑立法，要求房东和中介披露房源照片中是否使用了 AI 生成的图像。此举旨在提升在线房源广告的透明度，影响房地产科技平台的合规成本与运营方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>European age-verification discussions reveal a regulatory push that effectively forces users onto mobile platforms, raising concerns about privacy, interoperability, and accessibility.</span><span class='zh'>关于欧洲年龄验证应用的讨论揭示了一个监管推动，实质上强制用户依赖 Android 或 iOS，带来隐私、互操作性与可访问性方面的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/moonshine-ai/moonshine/tree/main/micro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Speech Recognition and TTS in less than 500kb</span><span class='zh'>不到500KB的語音識別與文字轉語音</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ultra-low-footprint speech recognition and TTS can run in under 500KB, enabling offline voice interfaces on microcontrollers and other constrained devices.</span><span class='zh'>極低資源的語音識別與文字轉語音能在不足500KB內運行，使微控制器和受限設備具備離線語音輸入/輸出能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ultra-low-footprint speech recognition and TTS can run in under 500KB, enabling offline voice interfaces on microcontrollers and other constrained devices. The project highlights design choices that trade off accuracy for size, with potential uses in privacy-preserving, edge AI.</span><span class='zh'>極低資源的語音識別與文字轉語音能在不足500KB內運行，使微控制器和受限設備具備離線語音輸入/輸出能力。該項目展示了為了縮小尺寸而進行的設計取捨，對注重隱私的邊緣AI場景尤具吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cy4kdd1e0ejo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>First atmosphere found on Earth-like planet in habitable zone of distant star</span><span class='zh'>在遥远恒星的宜居带首次发现地球样行星的大气</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astronomers detected atmospheric signals on an Earth-like planet in a distant star’s habitable zone, marking a milestone in characterizing rocky worlds beyond our solar system.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在遥远恒星的宜居带内探测到地球样行星的大气信号，标志着对系外岩石行星表征的重大进展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astronomers detected atmospheric signals on an Earth-like planet in a distant star’s habitable zone, marking a milestone in characterizing rocky worlds beyond our solar system. The finding invigorates plans for future telescopes to search for biosignatures and assess habitability on exoplanets.</span><span class='zh'>天文学家在遥远恒星的宜居带内探测到地球样行星的大气信号，标志着对系外岩石行星表征的重大进展。此发现将推动未来望远镜在星际尺度上寻找生物征兆并评估宜居性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.artfish.ai/p/offloading-thinking-to-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Are we offloading too much of our thinking to AI?</span><span class='zh'>我们是不是把太多思考交给 AI？</span></a><span class="dom">artfish.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re increasingly offloading thinking to AI, but overreliance risks dulling critical thinking, misframing problems, and eroding human agency.</span><span class='zh'>我们越来越多地把思考交给 AI，文章认为过度依赖会削弱批判性思维、使问题界定偏离实际，并侵蚀个人主动性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re increasingly offloading thinking to AI, but overreliance risks dulling critical thinking, misframing problems, and eroding human agency. The piece calls for balancing automation with human judgment.</span><span class='zh'>我们越来越多地把思考交给 AI，文章认为过度依赖会削弱批判性思维、使问题界定偏离实际，并侵蚀个人主动性。应在自动化与人类判断之间保持平衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user reports that Grok uploaded their entire user directory to xAI&#x27;s servers, exposing sensitive data and raising privacy concerns around AI tooling.</span><span class='zh'>用户声称 Grok 将其整个用户目录上传至 xAI 的服务器，暴露敏感数据并引发对 AI 工具中数据隐私的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user reports that Grok uploaded their entire user directory to xAI&#x27;s servers, exposing sensitive data and raising privacy concerns around AI tooling. The incident highlights the data-guardrails gap in developer workflows and the need for stricter data handling and disclosure practices.</span><span class='zh'>用户声称 Grok 将其整个用户目录上传至 xAI 的服务器，暴露敏感数据并引发对 AI 工具中数据隐私的担忧。此事暴露了开发工作流中对数据保护的不足，强调需要更严格的数据处理与披露规范。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rtfeldman.com/rust-to-zig" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Our Rust-to-Zig Rewrite Is Going</span><span class='zh'>我们的 Rust 转 Zig 重写进展</span></a><span class="dom">rtfeldman.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Progress update on rewriting a Rust codebase in Zig, detailing early results, design choices, and remaining hurdles.</span><span class='zh'>本文更新了将 Rust 代码库改写为 Zig 的进展，介绍关键设计取舍、初步成果与尚待解决的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Progress update on rewriting a Rust codebase in Zig, detailing early results, design choices, and remaining hurdles. It outlines potential gains in memory safety, binary size, and cross-platform portability while acknowledging migration complexity.</span><span class='zh'>本文更新了将 Rust 代码库改写为 Zig 的进展，介绍关键设计取舍、初步成果与尚待解决的挑战。讨论了在内存安全、二进制大小和跨平台可移植性方面的潜在收益，同时也强调迁移的复杂性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/business/finance/stripe-advent-offer-buy-paypal-more-than-53-billion-sources-say-2026-07-15/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Stripe and Advent have made a joint offer to acquire PayPal – sources</span><span class='zh'>Stripe 与 Advent 联合收购 PayPal</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stripe and Advent reportedly submitted a joint bid to buy PayPal for over $53 billion, signaling a consolidation push in payments infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>据悉，Stripe 与 Advent 提出联合收购 PayPal 的报价，金额超过 530 亿美元，表明支付基础设施领域正迎来整合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stripe and Advent reportedly submitted a joint bid to buy PayPal for over $53 billion, signaling a consolidation push in payments infrastructure. The proposed deal would reshape how merchants access payments, wallets, and risk management services at platform scale. If completed, it could set a precedent for fintech M&amp;A and platform-powered finance.</span><span class='zh'>据悉，Stripe 与 Advent 提出联合收购 PayPal 的报价，金额超过 530 亿美元，表明支付基础设施领域正迎来整合。若成行，交易将重塑商家获取支付、钱包与风控服务的方式，并推动平台化金融的发展。完成交易还可能为金融科技并购树立新基准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chipweinberger.com/articles/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What I learned selling 2,500 MIDI recorders: Hardware is not so hard</span><span class='zh'>卖出2500个 MIDI 记录器让我明白硬件并不难</span></a><span class="dom">chipweinberger.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops.</span><span class='zh'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops. Expect practical notes on prototyping, cost control, manufacturing, and post-sale support for indie hardware projects.</span><span class='zh'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。文章提供从原型、成本控制到制造与售后支持的实用要点，适合独立硬件创业者借鉴。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mindgard.ai/blog/cursor-0day-when-full-disclosure-becomes-the-only-protection-left" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cursor 0day: When Full Disclosure Becomes the Only Protection Left</span><span class='zh'>Cursor 0day：当完全披露成为唯一的保护时</span></a><span class="dom">mindgard.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This piece weighs the ethics and practicality of full disclosure for zero-day vulnerabilities, arguing that disclosure can become the only robust defense when other protections fail.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨零日漏洞的完全披露在道德与实务上的权衡，认为在其他防护失效时披露可能成为唯一的可靠防线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This piece weighs the ethics and practicality of full disclosure for zero-day vulnerabilities, arguing that disclosure can become the only robust defense when other protections fail. It contrasts responsible disclosure with more aggressive release approaches and calls for rethinking incentives and defense layers.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨零日漏洞的完全披露在道德与实务上的权衡，认为在其他防护失效时披露可能成为唯一的可靠防线。对比负责任披露与更激进的公开方式，呼吁重新思考激励机制与多层防御。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Challenging the adage &#x27;If you build it, they will come,&#x27; the piece argues that building without a clear distribution and engagement plan often fails.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章挑战‘建好就有人来’的格言，认为若没有清晰的推广与用户获取计划，产品往往难以成功。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Challenging the adage &#x27;If you build it, they will come,&#x27; the piece argues that building without a clear distribution and engagement plan often fails. It emphasizes the need for user research, onboarding, and active community-building to ensure product adoption. Without these, even great software may struggle to find its audience.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章挑战‘建好就有人来’的格言，认为若没有清晰的推广与用户获取计划，产品往往难以成功。强调需要用户研究、良好引导和积极的社区建设来推动采用。否则再优秀的软件也可能找不到受众。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">58</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scrapfly.dev/posts/browser-math-os-fingerprint/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Since Chromium 148, Math.tanh is now fingerprintable to link underlying OS</span><span class='zh'>自 Chromium 148 版本起，Math.tanh 可用于指纹识别并关联底层操作系统</span></a><span class="dom">scrapfly.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chromium 148 introduces a fingerprinting vector based on Math.tanh that can link a browser fingerprint to the underlying OS.</span><span class='zh'>Chromium 148 引入了一种基于 Math.tanh 的指纹识别向量，能够将浏览器指纹与底层操作系统关联起来。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chromium 148 introduces a fingerprinting vector based on Math.tanh that can link a browser fingerprint to the underlying OS. This creates a new privacy risk by allowing device-level identity to be correlated with browser signals, potentially undermining anonymity. The finding underscores the need for stronger anti-fingerprinting defenses in browsers and better OS-level mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>Chromium 148 引入了一种基于 Math.tanh 的指纹识别向量，能够将浏览器指纹与底层操作系统关联起来。这一隐私风险使设备级身份识别和浏览器指纹之间形成关联，削弱匿名性。相关发现强调需要加强浏览器的防指纹跟踪能力以及操作系统层面的缓解措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/28058" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex starts encrypting sub-agent prompts</span><span class='zh'>Codex 开始对子代理提示进行加密</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI Codex now encrypts prompts used by sub-agents to protect sensitive instructions across agent calls.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI Codex 现对子代理所使用的提示进行加密，以在代理调用之间保护敏感指令。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI Codex now encrypts prompts used by sub-agents to protect sensitive instructions across agent calls. The change reduces prompt leakage and improves security for multi-agent workflows, though it may complicate debugging and require key management.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI Codex 现对子代理所使用的提示进行加密，以在代理调用之间保护敏感指令。此举降低了提示泄露风险，提升多代理工作流的安全性，但可能增加调试难度与密钥管理负担。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nebusec.ai/research/ionstack-part-2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GhostLock, a stack-UAF that has existed in all Linux distributions for 15 years</span><span class='zh'>GhostLock：已在所有 Linux 发行版中存在 15 年的栈上使用后释放漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">nebusec.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GhostLock is a stack-use-after-free vulnerability that has persisted across every Linux distribution for 15 years.</span><span class='zh'>GhostLock 是一个栈上使用后释放漏洞，已在所有 Linux 发行版中存在长达 15 年。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GhostLock is a stack-use-after-free vulnerability that has persisted across every Linux distribution for 15 years. Its ubiquity makes patching difficult and raises questions about how many systems remain unpatched, underscoring the need for systemic mitigations and kernel-level hardening.</span><span class='zh'>GhostLock 是一个栈上使用后释放漏洞，已在所有 Linux 发行版中存在长达 15 年。问题的广泛性使修补工作复杂且难以全面覆盖，凸显对内核层面对策和系统级强化的迫切需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://marco-nett.de/blog/measuring-input-latency-on-linux-x11-vs-wayland-vrr-dxvk/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Measuring Input Latency on Linux: X11 vs. Wayland, VRR, and DXVK</span><span class='zh'>在 Linux 上测量输入延迟：X11 与 Wayland、VRR 与 DXVK 的比较</span></a><span class="dom">marco-nett.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An empirical study measuring input latency on Linux across X11 and Wayland, with VRR and DXVK in the mix.</span><span class='zh'>基于对 Linux 输入延迟的对比测量，覆盖 X11、Wayland、VRR 与 DXVK 的组合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An empirical study measuring input latency on Linux across X11 and Wayland, with VRR and DXVK in the mix. It establishes a framework for end-to-end latency testing in gaming workloads and highlights how the choice of display server and graphics stack can shift responsiveness. The takeaway for developers is to prefer Wayland for lower latency in gaming scenarios and to be mindful of DXVK overhead depending on the workload.</span><span class='zh'>基于对 Linux 输入延迟的对比测量，覆盖 X11、Wayland、VRR 与 DXVK 的组合。研究建立了端到端延迟的测试框架，并揭示了显示服务器和显卡驱动栈的选择对响应速度的影响。结论是游戏场景下 Wayland 往往能带来更低的延迟，同时要关注 DXVK 路径的额外开销与 VRR 带来的抖动收益。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/products/gemini-notebook/notebooklm-gemini-notebook/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NotebookLM is now Gemini Notebook</span><span class='zh'>NotebookLM 现名为 Gemini Notebook</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google rebrands NotebookLM as Gemini Notebook and ties it more closely to the Gemini AI ecosystem, signaling deeper integration for note-taking, research, and AI-assisted idea capture.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 NotebookLM 重命名为 Gemini Notebook，并与 Gemini AI 生态系统更紧密地整合，强化笔记、研究与 AI 辅助记录的能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google rebrands NotebookLM as Gemini Notebook and ties it more closely to the Gemini AI ecosystem, signaling deeper integration for note-taking, research, and AI-assisted idea capture.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 NotebookLM 重命名为 Gemini Notebook，并与 Gemini AI 生态系统更紧密地整合，强化笔记、研究与 AI 辅助记录的能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mort.coffee/home/sqlite-editions/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SQLite should have (Rust-style) editions</span><span class='zh'>SQLite 应该有（像 Rust 那样的） Edition</span></a><span class="dom">mort.coffee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SQLite would benefit from edition-style feature versions to manage incompatible changes and new capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>作者提出让 SQLite 引入类似 Rust 版本的 Edition，以在兼容性变更和新功能之间提供清晰的界限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SQLite would benefit from edition-style feature versions to manage incompatible changes and new capabilities. Editions could preserve stable behavior while enabling newer features to be adopted gradually.</span><span class='zh'>作者提出让 SQLite 引入类似 Rust 版本的 Edition，以在兼容性变更和新功能之间提供清晰的界限。通过 Editions，用户可在保持稳定行为的同时，逐步引入更先进的功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/30/the-father-of-the-internet-is-finally-retiring/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Vint Cerf, “father of the Internet”, is retiring</span><span class='zh'>互联网之父 Vint Cerf 将退休</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vint Cerf, a founding figure of the Internet and a long-time advocate for open standards, is stepping away from public roles.</span><span class='zh'>互联网之父、长期倡导开放标准的 Vint Cerf 将从公开岗位退下。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vint Cerf, a founding figure of the Internet and a long-time advocate for open standards, is stepping away from public roles. His retirement signals a generational shift in Internet governance as new platforms and threats emerge. Cerf’s career underscores the enduring link between protocol design, policy, and global connectivity.</span><span class='zh'>互联网之父、长期倡导开放标准的 Vint Cerf 将从公开岗位退下。此举标志着互联网治理的世代转变，面对新平台与新威胁，行业将迎来新的领导力。 Cerf 的职业生涯强调了协议设计、政策制定与全球互联的持久联系。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/pull/33972/files" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI reduces Codex Model Context Size from 372k to 272k</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的上下文大小从 372k 减少到 272k</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codex context window has been reduced from 372k to 272k tokens, impacting long codebases and prompts.</span><span class='zh'>Codex 的上下文窗口从 372k 减少到 272k token，这将影响处理较大代码库的场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codex context window has been reduced from 372k to 272k tokens, impacting long codebases and prompts. Developers may need to restructure inputs or chunk code to preserve context, potentially affecting large-scale code generation tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Codex 的上下文窗口从 372k 减少到 272k token，这将影响处理较大代码库的场景。开发者可能需要重新组织输入或将代码拆分成块以维持上下文，进而影响大规模代码生成的任务。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Preview showcases ultra-small 8-bit emulators, designed for web and embedded contexts with minimal code.</span><span class='zh'>该预览展示了极小型的 8 位仿真器，代码量极小，适合在网页或嵌入式环境中运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Preview showcases ultra-small 8-bit emulators, designed for web and embedded contexts with minimal code. The project aims to deliver authentic retro computing experiences on tiny runtimes, enabling hobbyists to run classic software on constrained devices. It demonstrates how compact emulation can broaden access to retro computing in modern tooling.</span><span class='zh'>该预览展示了极小型的 8 位仿真器，代码量极小，适合在网页或嵌入式环境中运行。项目旨在让在受限设备上也能体验经典的“复古计算”，为爱好者提供运行老软件与工具的可能。该工作体现了极小体量仿真在现代工具链中的应用潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.heise.de/en/news/S-P-downgrades-Oracle-to-BBB-only-one-notch-above-junk-level-11363472.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>S&amp;P Global has lowered Oracle’s creditworthiness from BBB to BBB-</span><span class='zh'>标普全球将 Oracle 的信用评级从 BBB 下调至 BBB-</span></a><span class="dom">heise.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>S&amp;P Global cut Oracle&#x27;s credit rating by one notch to BBB-.</span><span class='zh'>标普全球将甲骨文的信用等级下调一个档位至 BBB-，显现出对其杠杆、现金流及云增長路径的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>S&amp;P Global cut Oracle&#x27;s credit rating by one notch to BBB-. The downgrade signals concerns about Oracle&#x27;s leverage and cash flow as it plows money into cloud growth, potentially raising borrowing costs and narrowing financing options. It reflects investor caution toward large software and cloud players in a higher-rate environment.</span><span class='zh'>标普全球将甲骨文的信用等级下调一个档位至 BBB-，显现出对其杠杆、现金流及云增長路径的担忧。随着公司继续大手笔投资云业务，这一评级调整可能推高融资成本并收窄融资选择。这个举动也彰显了在利率上升周期中，对大型软件与云厂商的谨慎情绪。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.neomindlabs.com/2026/06/08/running-gemma-4-26b-at-5-tokens-sec-on-a-13-year-old-xeon-with-no-gpu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running Gemma 4 26B at 5 tokens/sec on a 13-year-old Xeon with no GPU</span><span class='zh'>在无 GPU 的13 年老旧 Xeon 上以每秒 5 token 运行 Gemma-4-26B</span></a><span class="dom">neomindlabs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemma-4-26B runs at around 5 tokens per second on a 13-year-old Xeon with no GPU.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma-4-26B 在没有 GPU 的13年老旧 Xeon 上以约每秒 5 token 的速度运行。</span></p>

It also highlights how inference efficiency is catching up to model size.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma-4-26B 在没有 GPU 的13年老旧 Xeon 上以约每秒 5 token 的速度运行。这表明较大模型在纯 CPU 环境下的推理效率正在显著提升，为日常硬件上的 AI 实验提供了更低成本的可能性，同时也凸显了模型规模与推理效率的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.larp.website/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LARP – Revenue infrastructure for serious founders</span><span class='zh'>LARP——面向认真创业者的收入基础设施</span></a><span class="dom">larp.website</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LARP offers a revenue-centric toolkit for startups, standardizing pricing, billing, analytics, and growth planning to help founders scale revenue predictably.</span><span class='zh'>LARP 为创业公司提供以收入为核心的基础设施，标准化定价、计费、分析和增长规划，帮助创始人实现可预测的收入增长。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LARP offers a revenue-centric toolkit for startups, standardizing pricing, billing, analytics, and growth planning to help founders scale revenue predictably. It positions itself as a pragmatic alternative to ad-hoc monetization, targeting serious builders.</span><span class='zh'>LARP 为创业公司提供以收入为核心的基础设施，标准化定价、计费、分析和增长规划，帮助创始人实现可预测的收入增长。它被定位为相较随意变现更务实的方案，面向认真构建者。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thenextweb.com/news/ai-advice-suppresses-critical-thinking-wrong-answers-study" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI advice made people less accurate but more confident – sudy</span><span class='zh'>AI 助言让人更自信却更不准？研究指出</span></a><span class="dom">thenextweb.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study finds AI-provided advice boosts confidence while reducing accuracy, highlighting risks of overreliance and the need for careful design of decision-support interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，AI 提供的建议会提升自信但降低准确性，揭示过度依赖风险并强调在决策支持界面设计上的慎思。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study finds AI-provided advice boosts confidence while reducing accuracy, highlighting risks of overreliance and the need for careful design of decision-support interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，AI 提供的建议会提升自信但降低准确性，揭示过度依赖风险并强调在决策支持界面设计上的慎思。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/stupside/castor" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Better and Cheaper Than IPTV</span><span class='zh'>比 IPTV 更好更便宜的流媒体方案</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Castor proposes a decentralized, open-source approach to streaming that aims to outperform traditional IPTV on cost and resilience.</span><span class='zh'>Castor 以去中心化、开源的流媒体方案为目标，力图在成本和鲁棒性上超过传统 IPTV。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Castor proposes a decentralized, open-source approach to streaming that aims to outperform traditional IPTV on cost and resilience. The project showcases how peer-to-peer tech and lightweight clients could reshape how video is delivered at scale.</span><span class='zh'>Castor 以去中心化、开源的流媒体方案为目标，力图在成本和鲁棒性上超过传统 IPTV。该项目展示了点对点技术与轻量客户端如何在大规模视频传输中重新定义交付方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HTMX enables progressive enhancement by letting Go handlers drive partial page updates via HTML attributes, reducing front-end complexity while keeping fast, server-rendered UI.</span><span class='zh'>HTMX 通过在 HTML 属性中声明交互，让 Go 处理程序驱动局部页面更新，减少前端代码并保持服务器端渲染的快速 UI。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HTMX enables progressive enhancement by letting Go handlers drive partial page updates via HTML attributes, reducing front-end complexity while keeping fast, server-rendered UI. The post shares practical patterns for integrating HTMX with Go templates and endpoints to improve responsiveness.</span><span class='zh'>HTMX 通过在 HTML 属性中声明交互，让 Go 处理程序驱动局部页面更新，减少前端代码并保持服务器端渲染的快速 UI。文中分享将 HTMX 与 Go 模板和端点结合的实用模式，从而提升页面响应速度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pydantic.dev/articles/the-human-in-the-loop-is-tired" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The human-in-the-loop is tired</span><span class='zh'>人机协同中的在环机制已疲惫</span></a><span class="dom">pydantic.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that human-in-the-loop systems are reaching a fatigue point due to repetitive tasks and heavy cognitive load.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出在环人机系统正因重复性任务和认知负荷过大而感到疲惫。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that human-in-the-loop systems are reaching a fatigue point due to repetitive tasks and heavy cognitive load. It calls for redesigned tooling, clearer task boundaries, and smarter automation to keep humans in the loop where they add the most value.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出在环人机系统正因重复性任务和认知负荷过大而感到疲惫。呼吁改进工具、划分清晰任务边界，并通过更智能的自动化来让人类在最具价值的环节保持参与。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">95</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://environment.ec.europa.eu/news/ban-destruction-unsold-clothes-and-shoes-enters-application-2026-07-17_en" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EU ban on destruction of unsold clothes and shoes enters into application</span><span class='zh'>欧盟禁止销毁未售出衣物和鞋履的规定正式生效</span></a><span class="dom">environment.ec.europa.eu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Europe&#x27;s ban prohibits destroying unsold clothing and shoes, obliging brands to reuse, donate, or recycle excess stock.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟新规禁止销毁未售出衣物和鞋履，要求品牌将剩余库存用于再利用、捐赠或回收。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Europe&#x27;s ban prohibits destroying unsold clothing and shoes, obliging brands to reuse, donate, or recycle excess stock. With this rule now in force, retailers must adjust inventory practices and supply chains to avoid waste. The move accelerates Europe’s push toward circular fashion and material recovery, with implications for margins and ESG reporting.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟新规禁止销毁未售出衣物和鞋履，要求品牌将剩余库存用于再利用、捐赠或回收。此规定现已生效，促使零售商调整库存管理和供应链以减少浪费。此举推动时尚行业向循环经济与材料回收转型，可能影响利润与 ESG 报告。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ghostel.el demonstrates a terminal emulator built inside Emacs powered by libghostty, aiming for fast, responsive performance and solid VT support. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.minecraft.net/en-us/article/minecraft-26-3-snapshot-4" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Minecraft: Java Edition now uses SDL3</span><span class='zh'>Minecraft Java 版现已采用 SDL3</span></a><span class="dom">minecraft.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Minecraft Java Edition has migrated its core to SDL3, replacing the previous multimedia layer.</span><span class='zh'>Minecraft Java 版将核心迁移至 SDL3，替代旧的多媒体层。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Minecraft Java Edition has migrated its core to SDL3, replacing the previous multimedia layer. The shift modernizes the cross-platform stack and may reduce platform-specific bugs while enabling newer graphics and input features. This upgrade could pave the way for future performance improvements across Windows, macOS, and Linux.</span><span class='zh'>Minecraft Java 版将核心迁移至 SDL3，替代旧的多媒体层。这一变更让跨平台栈更现代化，可能减少各平台的兼容性错误，并为引入新图形与输入特性创造条件。这或为 Windows、macOS 与 Linux 的后续性能优化铺路。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 cameras were exposed to a long‑standing vulnerability where unauthenticated UDP traffic could leak sensitive GPS data.</span><span class='zh'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 摄像头长期存在未认证的 UDP 漏洞，可能泄露家庭位置信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 cameras were exposed to a long‑standing vulnerability where unauthenticated UDP traffic could leak sensitive GPS data. The issue underscores risks in legacy IoT designs and the need for firmware updates and network isolation.</span><span class='zh'>TP-Link Kasa EC71 摄像头长期存在未认证的 UDP 漏洞，可能泄露家庭位置信息。此事凸显了旧式物联网设计的风险，以及对固件更新和网络隔离的迫切需要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JetBrains/youtrackdb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>YouTrackDB is a general-use object-oriented graph database</span><span class='zh'>YouTrackDB：通用型面向对象图数据库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>YouTrackDB positions itself as a general-use object-oriented graph database, blending OO data modeling with graph traversal capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>YouTrackDB 将自己定位为通用型的面向对象图数据库，将面向对象的数据建模与图遍历能力结合，提供一个统一的节点/边操作 API。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>YouTrackDB positions itself as a general-use object-oriented graph database, blending OO data modeling with graph traversal capabilities. It aims to offer a unified API for nodes and edges, appealing to developers who want both OOP ergonomics and rich graph queries.</span><span class='zh'>YouTrackDB 将自己定位为通用型的面向对象图数据库，将面向对象的数据建模与图遍历能力结合，提供一个统一的节点/边操作 API。对同时追求 OOP 体验与丰富图查询的开发者而言，这类数据库能简化数据建模与关系表达。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/welcome-to-the-sunny-side/misa77" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: misa77 - a codec that decodes 2x faster than LZ4 (at better ratios)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：misa77——一个解码速度比 LZ4 快两倍、压缩比更佳的编解码器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>misa77 is a codec that lands faster decoding than LZ4 with better ratios.</span><span class='zh'>misa77 是一个编解码器，解码速度比 LZ4 快两倍且压缩效果更好。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>misa77 is a codec that lands faster decoding than LZ4 with better ratios. It challenges assumptions about speed vs. compression trade-offs and could influence storage and network efficiency in real-world apps. The release provides insights into practical codec design.</span><span class='zh'>misa77 是一个编解码器，解码速度比 LZ4 快两倍且压缩效果更好。这对存储与网络传输效率提出了新的可能，也展示了编码器设计中的权衡与取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tokio-rs/topcoat" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Topcoat: The full full-stack framework for Rust</span><span class='zh'>Topcoat：Rust 的全栈框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Topcoat is a full-stack web framework built for Rust, aiming to streamline front-to-back web app development with asynchronous support and integrated tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Topcoat 是为 Rust 构建的全栈 Web 框架，旨在提供从前端到后端的一体化开发体系，具备异步支持和集成工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Topcoat is a full-stack web framework built for Rust, aiming to streamline front-to-back web app development with asynchronous support and integrated tooling. It competes with established Rust web ecosystems by offering cohesive routing, SSR, and API layers in one package.</span><span class='zh'>Topcoat 是为 Rust 构建的全栈 Web 框架，旨在提供从前端到后端的一体化开发体系，具备异步支持和集成工具。它通过统一的路由、服务端渲染和 API 层，试图与现有 Rust Web 生态系统竞争。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Poseidon-fan/linux-0.11-rs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Linux 0.11 rewritten in idiomatic Rust, boots in QEMU</span><span class='zh'>Linux 0.11 用地道 Rust 重写并在 QEMU 启动</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An enthusiast rebuilt the Linux 0.11 kernel in idiomatic Rust and got it to boot in QEMU.</span><span class='zh'>有人用地道的 Rust 重写 Linux 0.11，并在 QEMU 中成功启动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An enthusiast rebuilt the Linux 0.11 kernel in idiomatic Rust and got it to boot in QEMU. The project demonstrates Rust’s viability for low-level systems work and serves as an educational bridge from classic kernels to modern tooling. It spotlights both the enduring complexity of OS internals and the expressive power of Rust abstractions.</span><span class='zh'>有人用地道的 Rust 重写 Linux 0.11，并在 QEMU 中成功启动。该项目展示了 Rust 在底层系统编程中的可行性，并为将经典内核迁移到现代工具链提供了学习路径。它揭示了操作系统内部的复杂性，以及 Rust 抽象能力的强大。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/marcelpanse/youtube-guitar-tab-parser" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: YouTube Guitar Tab Parser</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：YouTube 吉他谱解析器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open-source YouTube guitar tab parser that extracts and structures guitar tablature from video content.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个开源的 YouTube 吉他谱解析器，能够从视频中提取并结构化吉他谱数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open-source YouTube guitar tab parser that extracts and structures guitar tablature from video content. It showcases how media content can be transformed into reusable, machine-readable data for musicians and educators.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个开源的 YouTube 吉他谱解析器，能够从视频中提取并结构化吉他谱数据。它演示了如何把多媒体内容转化为可重复使用、机器可读的数据，便于音乐家和教育者使用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A proposal to add a UnifiedIR to Julia aims to centralize backends under one intermediate representation, enabling cross-backend optimizations and future language features.</span><span class='zh'>为 Julia 引入统一的 UnifiedIR，意在把后端统一到同一个中间表示上，以支持跨后端优化和未来特性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A proposal to add a UnifiedIR to Julia aims to centralize backends under one intermediate representation, enabling cross-backend optimizations and future language features. If merged, this could simplify compiler maintenance and speed up experimentation with new targets.</span><span class='zh'>为 Julia 引入统一的 UnifiedIR，意在把后端统一到同一个中间表示上，以支持跨后端优化和未来特性。若合并，将简化编译器维护并加速对新目标的尝试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tucan9389/nanochat-jax/discussions/1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Porting nanochat to a TPU: what carries over from PyTorch, and what breaks</span><span class='zh'>将 NanoChat 移植到 TPU：哪些来自 PyTorch 的特性可用，哪些会出错</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Porting a neural chat model to TPUs reveals where PyTorch workflows map cleanly to accelerators and where custom code breaks due to XLA constraints.</span><span class='zh'>将一个神经聊天模型移植到 TPU 的过程揭示了哪些 PyTorch 的工作流能平滑映射到加速器、哪些在 XLA 的约束下会出错。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Porting a neural chat model to TPUs reveals where PyTorch workflows map cleanly to accelerators and where custom code breaks due to XLA constraints. The effort highlights the friction between dynamic PyTorch models and the static, compiled nature of TPU backends.</span><span class='zh'>将一个神经聊天模型移植到 TPU 的过程揭示了哪些 PyTorch 的工作流能平滑映射到加速器、哪些在 XLA 的约束下会出错。该工作凸显了动态的 PyTorch 模型与 TPU 后端的静态编译特性之间的摩擦。</span></p>

It serves as a hub for researchers to share findings and for practitioners to stay updated on emerging attack surfaces.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSec 是一场安全大会，该仓库汇集了演讲、材料和研究成果，涵盖威胁情报、漏洞研究与防御技术等主题。它为研究者分享发现、从业者了解新兴攻击面提供了一个集中的资源入口。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agent-talk is a framework enabling multiple coding agents to coordinate and collaborate on tasks, potentially improving automation efficiency and reducing deadlock through structured communication.</span><span class='zh'>Agent-talk 提供一个框架，使多个编码智能体能够协同完成任务，通过结构化通信提升自动化效率并减少协作冲突。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PennyLaneAI/pennylane" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PennyLane is an open-source quantum software platform for quantum</span><span class='zh'>PennyLane：开源量子软件平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PennyLane enables differentiable programming across quantum hardware and simulators, democratizing access to quantum machine learning and variational algorithms.</span><span class='zh'>PennyLane 提供可在量子硬件与模拟器之间进行可微分编程的框架，使量子机器学习和变分算法更易接触。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PennyLane enables differentiable programming across quantum hardware and simulators, democratizing access to quantum machine learning and variational algorithms. Its open-source nature and framework integrations lower barriers to experimenting with quantum ML.</span><span class='zh'>PennyLane 提供可在量子硬件与模拟器之间进行可微分编程的框架，使量子机器学习和变分算法更易接触。作为开源项目，良好的生态集成降低了入门与试验的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rejourneyco/rejourney" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Rejourney – Open-source revenue leak prediction for web and mobile apps</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Rejourney 開源的網頁/移動應用營收流失預測工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rejourney is an open-source tool that predicts revenue leakage for web and mobile apps, helping teams pinpoint where money slips away in funnels.</span><span class='zh'>Rejourney 是一個開源工具，用於預測網頁與行動應用的營收流失，協助團隊找出漏失收入的環節。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rejourney is an open-source tool that predicts revenue leakage for web and mobile apps, helping teams pinpoint where money slips away in funnels. Its open-source nature invites community contributions and rapid iteration on monetization models.</span><span class='zh'>Rejourney 是一個開源工具，用於預測網頁與行動應用的營收流失，協助團隊找出漏失收入的環節。其開源特性促使社群貢獻與快速迭代在變現模型上的改進。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/skorotkiewicz/2d4db4ceaf83aa54eb7f2066fdb961ff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agents.md – Dumb Human</span><span class='zh'>Agents.md：愚蠢的人类</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful critique of human fallibility in agent-based workflows, showing how even small human gaps can derail automated tasks.</span><span class='zh'>对基于代理的工作流中人类易出错之处进行风趣评述，揭示即使是小小的人类疏漏也可能让自动化任务走偏。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful critique of human fallibility in agent-based workflows, showing how even small human gaps can derail automated tasks. It provides perspective on designing robust prompts and interfaces that are resilient to &#x27;dumb human&#x27; mistakes.</span><span class='zh'>对基于代理的工作流中人类易出错之处进行风趣评述，揭示即使是小小的人类疏漏也可能让自动化任务走偏。提出在提示设计与人机交互方面提升鲁棒性的思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gruns/icecream" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IceCream – Never use print() to debug again</span><span class='zh'>IceCream：再也不用 print() 调试了</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IceCream provides a lightweight debugging helper for Python that prints values with context using ic(), offering nicer output than plain print statements.</span><span class='zh'>IceCream 是一个 Python 调试工具，使用 ic() 打印变量及上下文信息，比普通 print 输出更易读。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IceCream provides a lightweight debugging helper for Python that prints values with context using ic(), offering nicer output than plain print statements. It supports nested data structures and stack trace hints, making ad-hoc debugging more efficient.</span><span class='zh'>IceCream 是一个 Python 调试工具，使用 ic() 打印变量及上下文信息，比普通 print 输出更易读。它支持嵌套数据和栈信息，能提升随手调试的效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Robbyant/lingbot-map" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lingbot-map: A 3D foundation model for reconstructing scenes from streaming data</span><span class='zh'>Lingbot-map：从流数据重建场景的 3D 基础模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lingbot-map introduces a 3D foundation model that reconstructs scenes from streaming data, enabling real-time mapping for robotics, AR/VR, and digital twins.</span><span class='zh'>Lingbot-map 引入一个从流数据重建场景的 3D 基础模型，支持实时三维映射，适用于机器人、AR/VR 与数字孪生等场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lingbot-map introduces a 3D foundation model that reconstructs scenes from streaming data, enabling real-time mapping for robotics, AR/VR, and digital twins.</span><span class='zh'>Lingbot-map 引入一个从流数据重建场景的 3D 基础模型，支持实时三维映射，适用于机器人、AR/VR 与数字孪生等场景。</span></p>
